# ZeD feature map (Nov 18 2026, Build)

QA asked for this to build the new release test (`ZeDUI/qa/RELEASE_TEST.md`): every shipped feature with the file
that owns it, the function a script can call or wrap, how a player reaches it by mouse and by pad, one thing a PASS can
measure, and what the QA save may lack. It was read from the code on Nov 18 2026 (HEAD 667e9e9f0 and later docs only).
Every function, field and pad id below was found with grep; `file:line` references point at that day's code and drift
as the code changes, so search for the name when a line has moved.

## How to read the rows

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Feature | What the player sees, in plain words |
| Owning file | Under `42/media/lua/client/ZeD/` unless a path says otherwise; `ZeD-Loadouts/<file>` is the add-on ZeD-GUI: Loadouts' `42/media/lua/client/ZeDLoadouts/<file>` (1.0: Loadouts, outfits and Restock left ZeD for it; its rows stay here) |
| Entry function(s) | What a script calls or wraps to drive or watch it |
| Mouse path | The handler or hit id a click goes through |
| Pad path | The FocusManager zone and element ids, a wheel slice, or the game's own joypad focus where ZeD hands the pad to a panel |
| PASS measures | One state or size the script reads after acting |
| Needs | What the QA save may lack: server, admin, farm or animals, vehicle (and which), a specific item, another mod loaded, the main menu, death, a real controller; `-` for nothing |

## The Check column (QA, Nov 18 2026: the ledger)

Every feature row ends with **Check**: what tests it. `rt_<tag>` is the release-test script that covers it (one of
`ZeDUI/tools/qa/rt_*.lua`, listed in `release_test.txt`); `elsewhere: ...` names the run that covers it instead (the MP
kit, the main menu run, the compat run with its mods); `by hand: a real controller` and `not yet: ...` say why no
script can. `TODO` is a gap. `rt_coverage.ps1` reads this table before every release run and fails the run on a `TODO`,
on a row whose owning file or entry function is gone (a removed feature takes its row and check with it), and on a
ZeD file no row owns (a feature added without a row). Intake: a new feature's row goes in with the feature's commit;
QA writes its check before that beta's release run.

## Before writing pad checks

- **Pressing the pad with no pad:** the controller section opens with the recipe. Lock the input mode first
  (`Detector.setLockedMode("gamepad")`, or FocusManager's tick drops every hold), then `Focus.qaButton(name, true)`,
  `Focus.dispatchJoypadDown`, `qaButton(false)`, `qaButton(nil)`. The wheels, chords and run-sneak have their own qa
  hooks (listed there).
- **Not every surface is on FocusManager.** The main menu and the Options window take the pad through `MM.joyDown` /
  `MM.joyDir` (ZeD_MainMenu.lua); the Messages window, the chat window, the form card, the belt, the watch, the car
  radio, the seats, the siren, the reader and the guide take it on their own panel (`setJoypadFocus`). Their rows say
  which.
- **Only a real controller can check:** the LB + RB zoom chord, the aiming escape, held D-pad repeat, plugging a pad in
  or out, and the live lights on the Button map and test page. A few paths only hand the pad over when one is joined to
  the player (the wheels' Tabs, Belt and Dashboard slices, Place item's door guard, the door prompt's wrap).

## Facts the sweep turned up

- **Drawer widths:** a drawer is 220 or 480 design units, nothing else. There is no 260 width (the 260 in the code is
  the drawer's speed).
- **"+XP" over the head is the game's.** ZeD's part is the Messages line `ZeD.HaloText.onAddXP` writes.
- **Off by default:** Overhead notes and Moodle notices; a check turns them on and puts them back.
- **Mouse and pad agree on the open tab:** a click on the open Character tab folds its drawer, and so does the pad's A
  on it (QA K2-4, `_switchTabForNav`'s same-tab branch). They still differ on purpose on a skill row: a plain click
  does not pin it (pinning is in its menu, A or X on the pad).
- **Translation:** in game, a missing key comes back as itself, so `ZeD.tr(k) ~= k` is the check; the full check is
  `ZeDUI/tools/qa/i18n_guard.py`, outside the game.
- **One-surface see-through** cannot be read back (no way to read the alpha a window drew): its PASS is "no guarded
  error".

## Dead or unreachable (no check; listed so nobody writes one)

- `ContextMenuRegistry.togglePin`: saved pins apply, but nothing lets a player set one.
- `ItemActionManager.registerObserver`: no callers.
- The curtain's roll box on the Character and World windows: both use halves, so the side rolls by the pointer timer.
- `openTab` in ZeD_CharacterWindow.lua (replaced by Overrides/CharacterInfo.lua's).
- Animals: the Hunt mode is a stub.
- `QuickWheel.addGroups`, `openGroup`, `treat`; `ActionMap.fromKey`, `getBinding`, `getHoldConfig`; the `strip_*`,
  `layout_mode` and `inspect` actions are never dispatched.
- `cycleNext`, the `OnZeDThemeChanged` event, the glyph copy in modData, two empty sandbox-window methods.
- `ZeD_ZoneList`: never created.
- Small stale note: the Controller map's header says A or B closes it; only B does.

## Sections

1. Windows and tabs
2. Inventory, cards and menus
3. HUD and over the head
4. Vehicles, radios, maps and reading
5. The controller
6. Settings, look and safety
7. Multiplayer, main menu and add-ons


---

## Windows and tabs

Paths are relative to `42/media/lua/client/ZeD/`. CW = ZeD_CharacterWindow.lua, WW = ZeD_WorldWindow.lua, ORG = Organisms.lua (ZeD_Window), FM = Input/FocusManager.lua. Pad buttons can be faked with `ZeD.Input.Focus.qaButton(name, down, pn)` (FM:2751). Every tab strip stamps hit zones `"tab:<id>"` on its strip panel (`win._tabStripPanel` on Character, `win._actionTabStrip` on World); a test can click one with `strip:onMouseDown(z.x + 2, z.y + 2)` exactly as `openTab` does. Drawer widths are only ever 220 (half card / item card) or 480 (full card) design units; no 260 width exists in either window (260 appears only in the drawer speed constant `260 * 8`). Measure `win._drawerEffW` after the animation settles, against `ZeD.T.px(220)` / `ZeD.T.px(480)`.

### Equipment tab (Character)

---

## Inventory, cards and menus

Paths are under `42/media/lua/client/ZeD/`. "CW" = ZeD_CharacterWindow.lua, "WW" = ZeD_WorldWindow.lua, "IR" = InventoryRenderer.lua (table `ZeD.InventoryRenderer`). Pad zones are FocusManager ring zones on the window (`win._focusRing`): `charMainInv` / `worldMainInv` (rows), `charMC<n>` / `worldMC<n>` (Loot columns), `charItemCard` / `worldItemCard` (item card and object card), `charContainerStrip` / `worldContainerStrip`, `charCtl` / `worldCtl` (container band), `charTabContent` / `worldTabContent` (tab drawers). Inventory row ids are `invrow:<i>` (CW:1636), Loot column rows `mcrow:<slot>:<i>` (CW:1801). Mouse clicks on a list all go through `content.onMouseDown` (CW:~6860, WW:~5690) and its hit-zone ids.

### Object cards

All cards open in the World window drawer through `ZeD.ObjectCards.open(player, kindId, obj)` (ObjectCards.lua:151) -> `ZeD_WorldWindow.openObjectCard` (WW:6167); close `ZeD_ObjectCard:close` (ObjectCards:925) / `ZeD_WorldWindow.closeObjectCard` (WW:~6191); live card `ZeD_WorldWindow.getObjectCard(pn)`. Mouse: `ZeD_ObjectCard:_pointer` (1523) with zone `fid`s; pad: zone `worldItemCard`, element ids = the zone `fid`s (`buildFocusables` 1625), B = close. Width: `T.px(480)` (WW:79 `OBJECT_CARD_W`) or `T.px(220)` for `def.half` kinds (lock, preview).

## HUD and over the head

Paths are under `42/media/lua/client/ZeD/` unless noted. "Char wheel" = the Character wheel on D-pad Left (ZeD_DpadWheels.lua). The belt and the watch take the pad themselves (`setJoypadFocus` on the panel), not through FocusManager zones.

---

## Vehicles, radios, maps and reading

Paths are under `42/media/lua/client/ZeD/`. "Pad (engine focus)" means the window takes the controller with the game's own `setJoypadFocus` / `JoypadState.players[pn+1].focus` and reads buttons in its own `onJoypadDown` / `onJoypadDir*`; it has no FocusManager zone. "Pad (Focus)" means a FocusManager zone and element id. Data-only files with no behavior of their own (no rows): DashPx.lua, PhysPx.lua, SirenPlate.lua, MechFigs.lua (generated tables), ZeD_Plate.lua (the draw template every machine face uses), ZeD_GuideChapters.lua (chapter list). They are covered by the rows of the faces that draw them.

---

## The controller

Files are under `42/media/lua/client/ZeD/` unless a path says otherwise. `F` = `ZeD.Input.Focus` (Input/FocusManager.lua), `D` = `ZeD.DpadWheels`, `G` = `ZeD.WheelGesture`, `W` = `ZeD.Wheel`, `Q` = `ZeD.QuickChords`, `CW` = the Character window (`ZeD_CharacterWindow.getWindow(pn)`), `WW` = the World window (`ZeD_WorldWindow.getWindow(pn)`).

**How to press the pad with no pad (read before any row).**
- Lock the mode first: `ZeD.Input.Detector.setLockedMode("gamepad")` (Input/InputModeDetector.lua:358), and `setLockedMode(nil)` in the put-back. FocusManager's tick drops every hold outside gamepad mode (`_tickPlayer` mode gate, Input/FocusManager.lua:2989), and with no pad bound it drops them unless a QA button is held (`jid == nil and s.qaN == 0`, same function).
- A, X, Y, LB and RB are tap-or-hold, settled on the tick, so a press is three steps: `F.qaButton(name, true)` (FocusManager.lua:2751) then `F.dispatchJoypadDown(w, Joypad.<Btn>)` (:2506), wait frames, `F.qaButton(name, false)` (release = tap, or after 350 ms = hold), then `F.qaButton(name, nil)`. Names are JoypadButton fields: `"A"`, `"B"`, `"X"`, `"Y"`, `"LeftBump"`, `"RightBump"`. rt_kit wraps these as `RT.pad`, `RT.padDown`, `RT.padDir`.
- B and L3 / R3 act on `dispatchJoypadDown` alone. The D-pad is `F.dispatchJoypadDir(w, "up"|"down"|"left"|"right")` (:2697); `F.previewMove(w, dir)` (:2668) is a dry run that returns `{ zone, id, ... }` without moving.
- The player's way in is the window's own stub, `ZeD_Window:onJoypadDown(button, joypadData)` (Organisms.lua:1797) and `onJoypadDirUp/Down/Left/Right` (:1812-1830): they run `ZeD.padStart`, `F.passToCursor`, `F.padTarget`, then the dispatchers. Pass `joypadData = { player = pn }`.
- With no pad, `JoypadState.players[pn+1]` is nil, and some code checks it before it hands the pad over (noted per row).

---

## Settings, look and safety

Paths below are relative to `42/media/lua/client/ZeD/` unless they start with `shared/` (= `42/media/lua/shared/ZeD/`).
Every ZeD option is a `PZAPI.ModOptions` combo made at file load in `Settings.lua` (options id `ZeD_UI`); a combo is saved
by its item's position, item 1 Off / item 2 On for every switch. `opts.dict[id]` holds each option; `ZeD.Settings.<name>Option`
holds most of them directly.

Three ways a player changes a look setting, and every one calls the same setters, so a script can check all three agree:
- **Mouse, in game:** the window box at the right of any ZeD window title bar, `ZeD_Window:openWindowMenu` (Organisms.lua:1649), then `ZeD.WindowMenu.open` (ZeD_WindowMenu.lua:280). Its items come from `WM.build` (ZeD_WindowMenu.lua:162).
- **Pad, in game:** Select (Back/View) opens ZeD's wheel. The hook is on `ISBackButtonWheel` at ZeD_DpadWheels.lua:427, and the wheel's rows are built by `D.B.zed` (ZeD_DpadWheels.lua:645). The wheels go through the D-pad wheel code, not FocusManager.
- **ZeD setup:** page 1 of ZeD's Options window, `ZeD_MMOptions`. In game it is `MM.pauseOpts`, kind `options_ig`; at the main menu it is kind `options`.
  - Mouse: hit ids `<kind>:r<n>:seg<i>` sit in `self._hits` and are dispatched through `hitAt` (ZeD_MainMenu.lua:1130).
  - Pad: `MM.joyDown` / `MM.joyDir` (ZeD_MainMenu.lua:3090) call `ZeD_MMOptions:joyA/joyDir/joyX/joyY/joyBumper`. Focus ids are `orow:<n>`. This window is NOT driven through Input/FocusManager.lua.

### Dead or stub (no check)
- `cycleNext` (Settings.lua:1297): it says itself that nothing in ZeD calls it.
- The `OnZeDThemeChanged` event (shared/Tokens.lua:1323): it fires only if a file registers it, and none does.
- The glyph option's copy in modData (`md.ZeD_Gamepad.glyphDisplay`, Settings.lua:1125): it is written but never read.
- `ZeD_MMSandboxIG:snapshot` (:291) and `:next` (:505): both are empty on purpose.

## Multiplayer, main menu and add-ons

Paths are under `42/media/lua/client/ZeD/` unless shown (`shared/` = `42/media/lua/shared/ZeD/`). Line numbers are from the Nov 18 2026 tree.

Conventions used below:
- Admin tab: World window tab id `"admin"`, state `ZeD_WorldWindow.getWindow(pn)._adminTab` (`st`); a pushed card is `st.card` with `st.card.kind`; the tickets queue screen is `st.screen == "tickets"`.
- Server tab: Character window tab id `"server"`, state `ZeD_CharacterWindow.getWindow(pn)._serverTab`; cards the same way (`st.card.kind`).
- Mouse path for both tabs: the tab's `onClick` (AdminTab.lua:434 `A.onClick`, ServerTab.lua:522 `S.onClick`), which hands card clicks to `TC.cardClick` (ZeD_TicketCards.lua:2564) and list rows to `TC.listClick` (:824).
- Pad path for both tabs: FocusManager reads the tab's `buildFocusables` (AdminTab.lua:468, ServerTab.lua:544; cards via `TC.cardStops`, ZeD_TicketCards.lua:2652). Stop ids: a list row `"<flag>:tk:<id>"` (TC.itemStops :782, flag `adm` or `srv`), Admin tab buttons `"adm:b:<id>"`, picks `"adm:<id>"`, Server tab buttons `"srv:<id>"`.
- QA levers already in code: `ZeD.Admin.qaHold / qaLink / qaCaps` (ZeD_Admin.lua:18-20, 76, 82), `ZeD.Extras.qaHold / qaLink`, `ZeD.ServerPart.forceOff` (ZeD_ServerLink.lua), and the `qaOpen` functions listed per row.
- Main menu: `MM = ZeD.MainMenu`. A view is put up with `MM.showView(view)` (ZeD_MainMenu.lua:2825); its window is `MM.activeWindow()` (:2865) or `MM.root[<field>]`. Pad path at the main menu is not FocusManager: `MainScreen:onJoypadDown / onJoypadDir*` (Overrides/MainMenu.lua:248-276) call `MM.joyDown` (ZeD_MainMenu.lua:3090) / `MM.joyDir` (:3127), which call the active window's `joyA / joyB / joyX / joyY / joyBumper / joyDir`. Mouse path: the desk's pointer (`MM.pointer`, :1149) over each window's `_hits`.

Unconfirmed or notes:
- Pad stop prefixes confirmed: `TC.bind(win, st, "adm:tk:", ...)` (AdminTab.lua:335) and `"srv:tk:"` (ServerTab.lua:419). The Server extras row is `adm:tk:sum:extras`; the claim block buttons are `srv:faction` (ZeD_ClaimCards.lua:1115) and `srv:claim` (:1138).
- Chat `/` slash commands go through the game's `SendCommandToServer`; the console file says that the server answers it is unproven until the multiplayer run.
- Main menu "Mod load safety (Better Mod Experience)" in the Mod manager group is a pointer to its row under Other mods, not a separate feature.
